# The Tide Mother — Image Prompts for Grok Imagine (short clips, one image per clip)

*302 beats, 30:56 total. Every beat is **exactly 6 seconds**, the length Grok makes (only the narrated prologue beats run 10–15 s) and starts from **its own image**, so faces, costumes and voices never drift. Conversations are shot the natural cinematic way: the characters face each other, turned three-quarters to the camera so every face is visible, with two-shots and close-ups. Nights are truly dark, lit only by four fixed medieval light sources. Visuals follow the original FABLEON Visual Style Profile.*

**How to use:** generate every beat's image with the character sheets and the location sheet attached as references. Never chain beats from the last frame of the previous video; always start each beat from its own image.


---

## PROLOGUE — THE LAW OF KELVHAVEN


### Prologue — Kelvhaven


**Beat 001 · 0:00–0:06 · 6s · Opening hook**

- **Script segment:** "Black water, only drifting particles. A faint blue glow pulses somewhere far below. A huge luminous violet eye snaps open and stares straight into the lens."
- **Season:** Start of winter (the great storm): cold rain, wind and sea spray; no snow
- **Location sheet:** none (open sea or underwater)
- **Attach (location + character sheets):** none
- **Image prompt:** Photorealistic cinematic film still, dark fantasy, shot on 35mm, fine film grain, high-fidelity textures, shallow depth of field, natural bokeh, desaturated high-contrast color grade, 16:9. Extreme close-up, underwater: a single huge luminous violet slit-pupil eye opening in pitch-black water, faint faint violet veins around it. The moment in this clip: Black water, only drifting particles. Setting: deep underwater in a cold northern sea, drifting particles, kelp shadows, light fading from the surface far above. Lighting: darkness pierced by soft violet glow. Mood: ominous, mysterious. Palette: storm slate grey, fog grey, driftwood brown and wet-wool grey. No text, no watermark, no modern objects.

**Beat 002 · 0:06–0:21 · 15s · Dialogue and character emotion**

- **Script segment:** "NARRATOR: At the very edge of the northern sea sits a village most maps forget. Its name is Kelvhaven. And it lives by one strange rule."
- **Season:** Start of winter (the great storm): cold rain, wind and sea spray; no snow
- **Location sheet:** L0 Kelvhaven master aerial
- **Attach (location + character sheets):** L0 · none
- **Image prompt:** Photorealistic cinematic film still, dark fantasy, shot on 35mm, fine film grain, high-fidelity textures, shallow depth of field, natural bokeh, desaturated high-contrast color grade, 16:9. Aerial wide shot: a sweeping drone view skimming the grey northern sea at dusk toward black cliffs and a small fishing village on the bay. Setting: the northern sea at dusk under heavy grey cloud, black sea cliffs ahead and a small fishing village of grey fieldstone cottages with turf roofs on the bay. Lighting: the last cold light of dusk through heavy grey cloud, soft and diffused. Mood: mythic, mysterious, ominous. Palette: storm slate grey, fog grey, driftwood brown and wet-wool grey. No text, no watermark, no modern objects.

**Beat 003 · 0:21–0:36 · 15s · Dialogue and character emotion**

- **Script segment:** "NARRATOR: Three hundred years ago, its first families crawled ashore from a shipwreck with nothing but their nets. They built their homes from the stones the tide gave them."
- **Season:** Start of winter (the great storm): cold rain, wind and sea spray; no snow
- **Location sheet:** L0 Kelvhaven master aerial
- **Attach (location + character sheets):** L0 · Villagers
- **Image prompt:** Photorealistic cinematic film still, dark fantasy, shot on 35mm, fine film grain, high-fidelity textures, shallow depth of field, natural bokeh, desaturated high-contrast color grade, 16:9. Aerial shot, high angle: a timber harbor with clinker-built rowboats below grey fieldstone cottages with turf roofs. Setting: a cold northern fishing village seen from above, wind-bent grey fieldstone cottages with turf roofs, a timber harbor with clinker-built rowboats, fish drying racks, lobster pots and hanging nets. Lighting: flat diffused overcast storm light, soft and cold, sea spray in the air. Mood: mythic, mysterious, ominous. Characters: villagers in real, everyday medieval northern fishing-village clothing in many different natural colours, every person dressed differently: layered hand-woven wool tunics, kirtles and hooded cloaks in undyed cream, oatmeal and sheep-grey, walnut brown, faded woad blue, mustard ochre and rust brown, with sheepskin vests, oiled leather jerkins, oilskin capes, wool hoods and caps, leather belts, leg-wraps and worn leather shoes; all colours muted, earthy and weathered, nothing bright, among them the same recurring faces in every scene, always in the same clothes: Jory (about 18, lanky, messy blond hair, grey wool cap, faded woad-blue tunic, sheepskin vest), Ulf (big man in his 30s, shaved head, sheepskin vest over a rust-brown tunic, iron gaff), Sef (wiry man in his 30s, black beard, crooked nose, dark oiled-leather jerkin over a mustard-ochre tunic), Garth (about 60, bald, short grey beard, grey oilskin cape over an oatmeal tunic), Brenna (fishwife in her 40s, red-brown braid, walnut-brown kirtle, cream wool shawl) and Mags (about 70, white hair, sheep-grey kirtle, black wool shawl); everyone else keeps their hood up, faces in shadow; people of different heights and builds. The villagers are busy with their own everyday work, not posing and not watching the camera: fishwives gutting and salting herring at the stalls, people haggling and handing over coins, a man carrying a basket of fish on his shoulder, another rolling a barrel, an old woman mending a net on a stool, children weaving between the stalls, gulls fighting over scraps; people of different heights, facing in many different directions, in natural mid-movement. It is daytime: nobody carries any light. Nobody looks into the camera lens. Palette: storm slate grey, fog grey, driftwood brown and wet-wool grey. No text, no watermark, no modern objects.

**Beat 032 · 3:50–3:56 · 6s · Suspense and atmosphere**

- **Script segment:** "Chimney smoke drifts over turf roofs and boats bob in the harbor. Pale Maren walks down the lane with empty arms as villagers glance at her. Odile drops a basket of wet laundry and runs toward her."
- **Season:** Start of winter (the great storm): cold rain, wind and sea spray; no snow
- **Location sheet:** L3 The main lane
- **Attach (location + character sheets):** L3 · Maren, Odile, Villagers

Wide establishing shot: a cold fishing village under a grey sky, smoke from turf roofs, Maren walking alone down the harbor lane. The moment in this clip: Chimney smoke drifts over turf roofs and boats bob in the harbor. Setting: a cold northern fishing village, wind-bent grey fieldstone cottages with turf roofs, a timber harbor with clinker-built rowboats, fish drying racks, lobster pots and hanging nets. Lighting: flat diffused overcast storm light, soft and cold, sea spray in the air. Mood: somber, grieving. Characters: Maren, a woman about 30 with long wet tangled dark-brown hair with thin braids, pale weathered freckled skin, tired grey-green eyes, wearing a long black wool medieval dress with long sleeves and a full skirt to the ankles, a moss-green knitted wool shawl with a ragged fringed edge worn over both shoulders and fastened at the throat with a carved bone toggle (the shawl is clearly moss-green, with no black cape or hood over it), a braided brown rope belt with a long iron net-mending needle hanging from it, dark wool leg-wraps and simple brown leather sandals laced over them; Odile, a woman about 45 with grey-streaked hair and ruddy weathered cheeks, wearing a long faded brick-red wool dress with long sleeves, a thick faded red wool shawl with long fringes draped over her head as a hood and crossed over her chest, an oatmeal linen apron tied at the waist, and worn brown leather shoes; villagers in real, everyday medieval northern fishing-village clothing in many different natural colours, every person dressed differently: layered hand-woven wool tunics, kirtles and hooded cloaks in undyed cream, oatmeal and sheep-grey, walnut brown, faded woad blue, mustard ochre and rust brown, with sheepskin vests, oiled leather jerkins, oilskin capes, wool hoods and caps, leather belts, leg-wraps and worn leather shoes; all colours muted, earthy and weathered, nothing bright, among them the same recurring faces in every scene, always in the same clothes: Jory (about 18, lanky, messy blond hair, grey wool cap, faded woad-blue tunic, sheepskin vest), Ulf (big man in his 30s, shaved head, sheepskin vest over a rust-brown tunic, iron gaff), Sef (wiry man in his 30s, black beard, crooked nose, dark oiled-leather jerkin over a mustard-ochre tunic), Garth (about 60, bald, short grey beard, grey oilskin cape over an oatmeal tunic), Brenna (fishwife in her 40s, red-brown braid, walnut-brown kirtle, cream wool shawl) and Mags (about 70, white hair, sheep-grey kirtle, black wool shawl); everyone else keeps their hood up, faces in shadow; people of different heights and builds. The villagers are busy with their own everyday work, not posing and not watching the camera: fishwives gutting and salting herring at the stalls, people haggling and handing over coins, a man carrying a basket of fish on his shoulder, another rolling a barrel, an old woman mending a net on a stool, children weaving between the stalls, gulls fighting over scraps; people of different heights, facing in many different directions, in natural mid-movement. It is daytime: nobody carries any light. Nobody looks into the camera lens. Palette: storm slate grey, fog grey, driftwood brown and wet-wool grey. No text, no watermark, no modern objects.

## ACT II — THE SECRET


### Harbor market


**Beat 063 · 6:56–7:02 · 6s · Dialogue and character emotion**

- **Script segment:** "Gulls wheel over the busy fish market. Maren weaves through the crowd to Bram's stall. She sets a few copper coins on the salt-crusted plank."
- **Season:** Start of winter (the great storm): cold rain, wind and sea spray; no snow
- **Location sheet:** L2 Harbor market
- **Attach (location + character sheets):** L2 · Maren, Bram, Villagers
- **Image prompt:** Photorealistic cinematic film still, dark fantasy, shot on 35mm, fine film grain, high-fidelity textures, shallow depth of field, natural bokeh, desaturated high-contrast color grade, 16:9. Wide shot: a busy harbor fish market with gulls overhead, Maren approaching Bram's herring stall. The moment in this clip: Gulls wheel over the busy fish market. Setting: an open-air harbor fish market in a northern fishing village, salt-crusted plank stalls, herring in wicker baskets, gulls, hanging nets. Lighting: flat diffused overcast storm light, soft and cold, sea spray in the air. Mood: tense, suspicious. Characters: Maren, a woman about 30 with long wet tangled dark-brown hair with thin braids, pale weathered freckled skin, tired grey-green eyes, wearing a long black wool medieval dress with long sleeves and a full skirt to the ankles, a moss-green knitted wool shawl with a ragged fringed edge worn over both shoulders and fastened at the throat with a carved bone toggle (the shawl is clearly moss-green, with no black cape or hood over it), a braided brown rope belt with a long iron net-mending needle hanging from it, dark wool leg-wraps and simple brown leather sandals laced over them; Bram, a burly man about 40 with a scarred jaw, wet curly red-brown hair and a thick matted red-brown beard, wearing a heavy, shaggy, knee-length coat of worn brown and tan sheepskin fleece with a wide fleece collar, over a stained brown wool tunic with a wide leather belt, rough brown wool trousers and tall worn brown leather boots; villagers in real, everyday medieval northern fishing-village clothing in many different natural colours, every person dressed differently: layered hand-woven wool tunics, kirtles and hooded cloaks in undyed cream, oatmeal and sheep-grey, walnut brown, faded woad blue, mustard ochre and rust brown, with sheepskin vests, oiled leather jerkins, oilskin capes, wool hoods and caps, leather belts, leg-wraps and worn leather shoes; all colours muted, earthy and weathered, nothing bright, among them the same recurring faces in every scene, always in the same clothes: Jory (about 18, lanky, messy blond hair, grey wool cap, faded woad-blue tunic, sheepskin vest), Ulf (big man in his 30s, shaved head, sheepskin vest over a rust-brown tunic, iron gaff), Sef (wiry man in his 30s, black beard, crooked nose, dark oiled-leather jerkin over a mustard-ochre tunic), Garth (about 60, bald, short grey beard, grey oilskin cape over an oatmeal tunic), Brenna (fishwife in her 40s, red-brown braid, walnut-brown kirtle, cream wool shawl) and Mags (about 70, white hair, sheep-grey kirtle, black wool shawl); everyone else keeps their hood up, faces in shadow; people of different heights and builds. The villagers are busy with their own everyday work, not posing and not watching the camera: fishwives gutting and salting herring at the stalls, people haggling and handing over coins, a man carrying a basket of fish on his shoulder, another rolling a barrel, an old woman mending a net on a stool, children weaving between the stalls, gulls fighting over scraps; people of different heights, facing in many different directions, in natural mid-movement. It is daytime: nobody carries any light. Nobody looks into the camera lens. Palette: storm slate grey, fog grey, driftwood brown and wet-wool grey. No text, no watermark, no modern objects.

### Cave mouth — the mob


**Beat 184 · 19:02–19:08 · 6s · Major climax**

- **Script segment:** "A line of torches at a sea cave mouth at low tide, villagers pushing in, a horse-sized sea dragon rising behind a woman."
- **Season:** Early winter chill: cold wind, grey skies, drizzle and sea mist; no snow, no ice
- **Location sheet:** L11 The hidden cove
- **Attach (location + character sheets):** L11 · Villagers, Tal (adult sheet), Maren
- **Image prompt:** Photorealistic cinematic film still, dark fantasy, shot on 35mm, fine film grain, desaturated high-contrast color grade, 16:9. REAR VIEW. High drone shot from behind and above a running mob, looking forward over their heads along a long dark black-sand beach toward a sea cave far away. This is a rear tracking shot: the camera is BEHIND the mob, so we see ONLY THE BACKS of the villagers. LOWER HALF OF THE FRAME (closest to the camera): about twenty-five villagers running AWAY from the camera, toward the top of the frame. We see the backs of their heads, raised hoods, the backs of their cloaks and coats, and the soles of their boots lifting as they run. No villager's face is visible. Their cloaks and the smoke of their pitch torches stream BACKWARD toward the camera from the speed of running. They hold the pitch torches raised high and forward, at uneven heights, so the orange light falls on the wet sand IN FRONT of them, and their backs are dark silhouettes rimmed with orange light. A long trail of fresh footprints in the wet sand runs from the bottom edge of the frame up to the mob. At the very front, the broadest back of all: a big man with curly red-brown hair in a heavy, shaggy brown and tan sheepskin fleece coat, an iron harpoon held up in his right hand. UPPER THIRD OF THE FRAME (about 70 meters away, small because of the distance): the dark mouth of a sea cave at the far end of the beach. In front of it stands a lone woman in a long black wool kirtle and black hooded mantle, FACING THE CAMERA and the oncoming mob, arms spread. Just behind her shoulder stands a horse-sized sea dragon, his head level with hers, blush-pink pearly scales, faint glowing violet eyes and violet veins. These two are the only faces in the image, small and far away. Between the mob and the cave: 70 meters of empty dark wet sand reflecting the torchlight. TRUE NIGHT: deep darkness, black sky, a thin moon behind cloud. The only light is the moving orange torchlight around the mob and the faint violet glow at the far cave. Early winter chill, drizzle, sea mist, no snow, no ice. Palette: storm slate grey, fog grey, driftwood brown and wet-wool grey, with orange pitch torches against cold blue darkness. No faces on the villagers, no one looking at the camera, no text, no watermark, no modern objects. Fixed medieval light sources, same design in every scene: pitch torches are wooden staves with rag heads soaked in fish oil and pitch.
